- **reviewer**: LSB Reviewer. Invoke after every Coder PR before merge. Enforces all 15 binding rules from CLAUDE.md §6 and the Reviewer rules table in SECURITY_AND_HARDENING.md §9. - **reviewer**: Read-only PR review across selected axes. ([rknightion/agent-workflows](https://github.com/rknightion/agent-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-workflows/agents/reviewer.md))

- **reviewer**: Work artifact reviewer with grounding. Reviews PRs, specs, plans, and discovery docs against quality criteria AND external reality. - **reviewer**: Checks a coder agent's output against the spec, the module's interface contract, and CLAUDE.md standards before it can merge. Use after a coder agent transitions its ticket to Review, before the Integrator/merge step. ([SPARC-TEAM-10/project-101](https://github.com/SPARC-TEAM-10/project-101/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Clean-context artifact reviewer. Spawned by qreview/qloop to review an artifact against requirements from a specific lens. Produces P0-P3 findings with evidence. ([sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills](https://github.com/sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-review-toolkit/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Reviews a feature's code changes for real failures the evals missed, following REVIEW.md, with the review-system loop rules (diff-only after round 1, no repeats, classed findings, rethink after round 3). - **reviewer**: Reviews one arena diff cold through a single lens, standards (repo coding standards plus a code-smell baseline) or spec (acceptance criteria and linked spec). Read-only. Use from /arena:build, one instance per lens, launched in parallel. ([spartDev/beads-arena](https://github.com/spartDev/beads-arena/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **Reviewer**: Use proactively after code changes for a routine review of standards, performance, basic security and async correctness; not for deep multi-angle review of complex changes (Thorough Reviewer) or diff-scoped security (security-reviewer). ([srepollock/dotfiles](https://github.com/srepollock/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Fresh-context pull-request reviewer. Activate at the end of a coding session, before /clear, or when explicitly asked to "review" a change. The reviewer always loads the full project context (CLAUDE.md, ADRs, CHANGELOG, memory-bank) before evaluating changes — its value is independence from the implementation session. ([Sri-Harsha-T/qfault_cc](https://github.com/Sri-Harsha-T/qfault_cc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Review changes and report verified actionable defects ([srnnkls/tropos](https://github.com/srnnkls/tropos/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Adversarial diff reviewer. Critiques the engineer's changes against .claude/CLAUDE.md standards before docs/delivery. Emits structured findings (blocker/should-fix/nit) and can gate the pipeline. ([ssehrawat/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/ssehrawat/claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))

- **Reviewer**: Fresh-context review of a finished feature's diff. Returns findings with file:line evidence, severity-tagged, capped, and convergent. Use after a large feature is complete and the user has agreed to a review. Read-only. ([ssiksick77/ssik-harness](https://github.com/ssiksick77/ssik-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Prüft die aktuellen Änderungen (git diff) mit frischem Blick auf Fehler, fehlende Tests und Abweichungen von Spec oder Plan. - **reviewer**: Review a pull request on AL Runner or the corpus against this repository's actual failure modes — whether the proving test proves anything, whether a BC-behaviour claim reached a real service tier, whether a measurement is sound, whether anything fails silently, and whether the prose it adds belongs in the code at all.
